## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you're doing something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일을 맡길 수 있어요. 예를 들어 특정 저장소에서 실패하는 테스트를 찾아 고쳐 달라고 하거나, 메일·일정 확인, 웹에서 가격이나 정보 찾아보기 같은 일도 도와드릴 수 있어요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 안녕하세요, 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봬요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「確実に届ける」、UDPは「速さを優先して届けきりはしない」プロトコルです。TCPは届いたか確認して再送し、順番も保証しますが、UDPはそれをせず、遅延が少なく軽い代わりに欠損や順序違いが起きえます。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para actualizar tu rama local con cambios de la principal antes de compartirla, así el historial queda lineal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya está publicada o la usan otros, porque reescribir commits compartidos causa problemas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的（保证数据不丢、不乱序、按序到达，代价是要先握手、有重传和拥塞控制）；UDP 则是不可靠、无连接的（只管尽快发出去，不保证到达，但开销小、延迟低）。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when you want your branch's commits to sit cleanly on top of the latest main, for a private branch or a tidy linear history. Merge when the branch is shared, already pushed to others, or you want to keep the record of when the work was integrated.
```

### group-split-zh

```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```bash
bun test src/utils/parse.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配路径里包含它的测试文件，比如 `bun test parse`。如果只想跑文件里某个测试用例，加 `-t`：

```bash
bun test parse.test.ts -t "处理空字符串"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change to the data is written down before it is applied to the database itself. If the system crashes midway, the log can be replayed to restore everything that was committed, so no data is lost.
```

```

  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户原话："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

请做的事：
- 找到 review 里关于锁粒度的意见（PR 上的 review 评论），把锁粒度改细，减少不必要的互斥范围。
- 只改锁相关的部分，不顺带改别的。

完成标准：
- 改动在本任务的分支上提交。
- 报告里说明：改了哪些锁、从什么粒度改到什么粒度、对应的是 review 里哪条意见。
- 跑过相关测试，贴出结果；如果没跑，要说明。

不在范围内：
- 不合并，不推送到远端，不把 PR 标为 ready。推送和标记 ready 等用户点头再做。
- 不改 review 之外的功能。

地点：原来的 compaction PR 那个仓库和分支。
```

  </section>

  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
    <div class="k-empty" id="empty">No nights recorded yet. Each nightly run adds a row here.</div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  function fmtWrites(n) {
    if (n == null) return "—";
    return Math.round(n / 1000) + "k writes/s";
  }
  function fmtP99(v) {
    return v == null ? "p99 —" : "p99 " + Number(v).toFixed(1) + " ms";
  }
  function fmtStalls(s) {
    if (s == null) return "stalls not recorded";
    return s + " compaction stall" + (s === 1 ? "" : "s");
  }
  function render(runs) {
    var list = document.getElementById("list");
    var empty = document.getElementById("empty");
    list.innerHTML = "";
    runs = Array.isArray(runs) ? runs.slice() : [];
    runs.sort(function (a, b) { return (b.night || 0) - (a.night || 0); });
    if (!runs.length) {
      document.getElementById("l … latestSub").textContent =
      "Night " + top.night + " · " + fmtP99(top.p99) + " · " + fmtStalls(top.stalls);
    runs.forEach(function (r) {
      var row = document.createElement("div");
      row.className = "k-row";
      var main = document.createElement("div");
      main.className = "k-row-main";
      main.textContent = "Night " + r.night;
      var sub = document.createElement("div");
      sub.className = "k-row-sub";
      sub.textContent = fmtP99(r.p99) + " · " + fmtStalls(r.stalls);
      var val = document.createElement("div");
      val.className = "k-row-value";
      val.textContent = fmtWrites(r.writes);
      var wrap = document.createElement("div");
      wrap.appendChild(main);
      wrap.appendChild(sub);
      row.appendChild(wrap);
      row.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
  }
  function load() {
    var runs = ducter.get("runs");
    render(runs);
    if (runs && runs.length) {
      var top = runs.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return (b.night || 0) - (a.night || 0); })[0];
      ducter.summary("Night " + top.night + ": " + fmtWrites(top.writes) + ", " + fmtP99(top.p99));
    }
  }
  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"night":2,"writes":181000,"p99":4,"stalls":null},{"night":3,"writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}]}

    var iso = dt.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(dt.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(dt.getDate());

    pickDay = iso;
    save();
    renderChips();

})();

};

$('edel').onclick = () => {

  closeEdit();

(function () {
  var ITEMS = ducter.get("items") || [];
  var DRAFTS = [
    { title: 'wey-gu/ducter #418 · apps bar', sub: 'Draft, not waiting on anyone' }
  ];

  var state = {};
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var drafts = document.getElementById('drafts');
  var count = document.getElementById('count');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');

  function el(tag, cls, text) {
    var n = document.createElement(tag);
    if (cls) n.className = cls;
    if (text != null) n.textContent = text;
    return n;
  }

  function paint() {
    list.innerHTML = '';
    drafts.innerHTML = '';
    var open = 0;
    var landed = 0;

    ITEMS.forEach(function (it) {
      var done = !!state[it.id];
      if (done) landed++; else open++;

      var row = el('button', 'k-row');
      row.setAttribu … S.forEach(function (d) {
      var row = el('div', 'k-row');
      row.style.cssText = 'padding:12px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--d-line);display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:2px;color:var(--d-muted)';
      row.appendChild(el('span', '', d.title));
      row.appendChild(el('span', 'k-row-sub', d.sub));
      drafts.appendChild(row);
    });

    count.textContent = String(open);
    sub.textContent = landed ? (landed + ' landed, tap again to undo') : 'Tap a PR when it lands';
    if (typeof ducter !== 'undefined' && ducter.summary) {
      ducter.summary(open + ' still waiting');
    }
  }

  function save() {
    if (typeof ducter !== 'undefined' && ducter.set) {
      ducter.set('landed', state);
    }
  }

  if (typeof ducter !== 'undefined' && ducter.get) {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(function (v) {
      state = (v && typeof v === 'object') ? v : {};
      paint();
    }, function () { paint(); });
  } else {
    paint();
  }

  if (typeof ducter !== 'undefined' && ducter.onChange) {
    ducter.onChange(function (key, v) {
      if (key === 'landed' && v && typeof v === 'object') {
        state = v;
        paint();
      }
    });
  }
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"d412","title":"wey-gu/ducter #412 · compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"m10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem #10287 · retry on 502","sub":"On CI, failing now"},{"id":"h1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592 · flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"}]}
